BEFORE YOU EXPLORE

What to expect

The imported checklist contains 9,284 source rows across 175 published sections. The largest is Base Set - Grandstand (100 source rows). Pack odds are not available in this import; checklist size and serial numbering do not establish pull chances. Published parallel or numbering details are available in the family views.

THE DETAILS MATTER

Official data.
Clear expectations.

“1 in 24 packs” means a card from that category, across all eligible subjects. It is not the chance of pulling a particular player. Serial numbering is a print run, not a pack hit rate.

Missing values are not estimates. Original manufacturer columns and notes remain available in card details. A listed subject is not guaranteed in every parallel. Imported odds categories alone do not establish an exhaustive parallel inventory.

All 9,284 rows from the official Panini CSV export are retained, including all original columns, literal card-set parallel names and SEQUENCE values. Empty numbering is not an estimate. Pack odds are absent from this export. Product year does not establish a release date.
